金利上昇による企業の負担増加は 
いつ本格化するか

○ 日銀の政策金利や、金融市場で決まる国債金利に比べ、企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇はまだ
限定的。企業の新規借入金利や負債残高全体への影響波及に時間がかかることが背景 
○ 金融機関の新規貸出金利は、概ね3カ月かけて政策金利の変化を反映する傾向。特に地域金融機関
で上昇がやや遅く、企業規模により有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが異なる一因に 
○ 借入・社債金利の動きを踏まえると、企業の利払い負担増加は2026～2028年度に本格化し、年間
約3兆円の利益下押し要因に。これまで以上に資本効率を意識した経営が求められる

１．政策金利や国債金利が上昇するも、企業の利払い負担増加はまだ本格化せず 
日本銀行は2026年9月の金融政策決定会合で、 政策金利を1.0％から1.25％へ引き上げた。これは1995
年以来、31年ぶりの高水準である1。また、金融市場で決まる10年国債金利（いわゆる長期金利）は、
2026年9月に節目となる3％を超えた。こちらも1996年以来30年ぶりの高さであり、政策金利や国債金
利はまさに歴史的な転換点を迎えている。 
一方で、 企業が実際に負担する金利の上昇は今のところ限定的だ。 図表1は、 企業の有利子負債利子
率 （＝支払利息等÷有利子負債残高） を指標金利 （政策金利、10年国債金利）と比較したものである。

### Page 2

図中の凡例にある数字は、 各金利についてマイナス金利政策解除 （2024年3月19日 ）前 の2023年10～12
月期と、直近の2026年4～6月期を比較した値だ。政策金利が＋0.93％Pt、10年国債金利が＋1.80％Pt
と大きく上昇した一方で、企業の有利子負債利子率は＋0.42％Ptと上昇幅が小さく、企業の利払い負
担の増加はまだ本格化していない。実際、日銀短観における企業の資金繰りに関する調査（図表2）を
みると、「楽である」の回答率から「苦しい」の回答率を差し引いたDIが、2021年以降プラス圏で概
ね横ばいを維持している。企業の実感としても、これまでに実施された複数回の利上げや、金融市場
における国債金利の大幅な上昇は、まだ資金調達環境の悪化につながっていないようだ。 
なぜ、こうしたギャップが生じるのか。主な要因として考えられるのは次の2点である。 
第一の要因は、指標金利と企業の新規調達金利とのタイムラグだ。政策金利などの指標金利が変化
してから、企業が新たに資金を調達する際の金利が変化するまで、少し時間がかかる。特に、企業の
有利子負債の半分強を占める金融機関からの借入において（図表3）、新規調達金利のタイムラグが生
じやすいと考えられる。 
第二の要因は、企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグだ。企業が
新たに資金調達する際の金利が上昇しても、それが有利子負債残高全体にすぐ反映されるわけではな
い。 企業の金融機関借入金の4～5割程度が固定金利型であるほか2、 主に大企業が発行する社債の多く
が固定利付債であり、過去の低金利時代に調達した負債がまだ残っているためだ。 
本稿では、 これら2つの要因を踏まえて、政策金利・国債金利などの指標金利上昇を受けた企業の利
払い負担増加が、いつ本格的に現れるかを試算する3。 
２．金融機関からの新規借入金利は、地域金融機関で上昇ペースが遅い傾向 
まず、第一の要因（指標金利から企業の新規調達金利へのタイムラグ）の影響を検証しよう。図表
3に示したように、 企業の有利子負債には金融機関などからの借入金と、 主に大企業が発行する社債が
ある。このうち、社債を新規発行する際の金利は概ね国債金利に連動して決まり、タイムラグがほぼ
図表 3 企業の有利子負債残高の内訳 図表 4 金融機関業態別の新規貸出金利

含まれるが、 シェアが小さい （0.1％程度） ため表示し

無いため、ここでの検証は金融機関などから新規に借り入れる際の金利が対象になる。 
図表4は、金融機関の新規貸出金利（金融機関からみた貸出）を、業態別に示したものだ。都市銀行
では新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが速い一方、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では政策金利の上昇に比べて新
規貸出金利の上昇ペースがやや遅い。こうした地域金融機関における金利上昇の遅れが、指標金利と
企業の新規調達金利のタイムラグに影響していると考えられる。 
金融機関の業態によって新規貸出金利の上昇ペースに差があるのは、貸出の金利形態別シェアが異
なることが一因であろう。大手行では企業向け貸出全体の約6割を占める変動金利型貸出のうち、ほと
んどが「市場金利連動」であるため、金融市場の変化による影響がすぐに反映されやすい。他方で、
地方銀行や信用金庫では変動金利型貸出のうち「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが高い。短期プ
ライムレートとは、各金融機関が設定する企業向け貸出の最優遇金利である。政策金利の変更などを
反映して決められることが多いが、各金融機関の競争環境などによって反映時期・度合いに差が生じ
る4。これにより、第二地方銀行や信用金庫では都市銀行に比べて新規貸出金利の上昇ペースが遅くな
る傾向があると考えられる5。 
こうした金融機関業態別の違いは、企業サイドにおいて、有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが規模別
に異なる一因になっている可能性がある。足元の金利上昇局面では、都市銀行からの借入が多いと考
えられる大企業（資本金10億円以上）で有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースが速く、信用金庫など地域金
融機関からの借入が多いと考えられる小規模企業（同1千万円未満）で上昇ペースが遅い（図表5）。
2010年代の金利低下局面では大企業の有利子負債利子率が小規模企業を下回る傾向にあったが、足元
では完全に逆転している。大企業は社債が有利子負債残高の約3分の1を占めるため、金融機関の新規
貸出金利のみが原因とは言えないものの、金融機関業態別の違いが企業規模別の資金調達環境に影響
を及ぼしている可能性がある点は興味深い。 
では、今後日銀がさらに利上げを進めていくと、金融機関の新規貸出金利はどの程度のペースで、
どこまで上昇していくか。過去の政策金利と新規貸出金利の関係を統計的に分析すると、 政策金利1％

平均値で除した値、対象は金融・保険を除く全産業

### Page 4

4

Ptの上昇は新規貸出金利を当月に0.31％Pt押し上げ、その後さらに3カ月間かけて当月からの累計で
0.85％Pt押し上げる傾向があることが明らかになった。日銀が今後2027 年半ばにかけて政策金利を
2.0％まで引き上げていくことを前提にすると6、金融機関の新規貸出金利（2026年8月時点で1.78％）
は2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度へ上昇すると試算される （図表6） 。 マイナス金利政策の解除前と
比較すると、2％Pt弱の上昇になる計算だ。 
３．企業の利払い負担増加は今後 2028 年度にかけて本格化。 利益を年間約 3 兆円下押し 
次に、第二の要因（企業の新規調達金利と有利子負債残高全体の利払い負担とのタイムラグ）の影
響を検証しよう。金融機関の新規貸出金利や社債の新規発行金利の上昇が、それぞれの残高全体にど
の程度のペースで反映されるかを確認し、企業の利払い負担がいつ本格化に増加するか試算する。 
金融機関の新規貸出金利と貸出残高全体の金利を比較すると、今回の金利上昇局面では新規貸出金
の上昇に対して貸出金利残高全体の金利上昇がやや遅れている（図表7）。 先に述べたように、金融機
関の貸出の一部が固定金利型であり、新規貸出や借り換えに伴って新規貸出金利の上昇が残高全体へ
波及するのに時間がかかるためだ。 
両者の過去の関係を統計的に分析すると、新規貸出金利の上昇がおよそ5年程度かけて残高全体の金
利を緩やかに押し上げる傾向があるとの結果になった。 図表6で試算したように、 先行きの新規貸出金
利が2027年秋ごろにかけて2.65％程度まで上昇していくと想定すると、貸出残高全体の金利（2026年
8月時点で1.51％ ）は2027年末に2.0％程度、2028年末に2.3％程度と緩やかに上昇し、2030年代前半に
は新規貸出金利と同じ2.65％程度へ収束していく計算になる。 
同様に社債について、 新発債金利と既発債金利を比較したものが図表8だ。 新発債金利は2022年ごろ
から上昇して足元では2％台後半に達しているが、既発債金利はまだ1％強にとどまっており、金融機
関の貸出金利に比べると残高全体への反映に時間がかかりやすいことがうかがえる。なお、日本で新

### Page 5

5

規に発行される社債の平均年限は現在5～6年であり、新発債金利は概ね5 年国債金利に連動して動く
傾向があることから、先に述べたように指標金利→新発債金利のタイムラグはほとんど生じない。 
今後、社債残高が物価上昇にあわせて年間＋2％のペースで増加していくとの前提を置き、 先行きの
償還に伴う借り換えや追加的な新規発行が行われていく場合、既発債金利は2030年代初めにかけて年
間＋0.3％Pt程度の緩やかなペースで上昇していくと試算される（新発債金利は5年国債金利の先行き
予測値に連動すると想定）。7年債や10年債といった年限が長めの社債も一定の規模があることから、
新発債金利の上昇が既発債金利に反映されるまでに時間を要する結果になった。 
最後に、金融機関の貸出金利（企業からみた借入金利7）と社債金利の試算結果を用いて、企業の有
利子負債利回りへの影響を検証する。企業の有利子負債に占める借入と社債の金額シェアを先行き一
定として、 貸出金利と社債金利の変化を有利子負債利子率に反映したものが図表9だ。2023～2025年度
の有利子負債利子率の上昇ペースは年間＋0.1～＋0.2％Pt程度と緩やかだったが、2026～2028年度に
は同＋0.3～＋0.4％Ptに加速すると見込まれる。企業の有利子負債の多くを占める借入金の金利上昇
が、主な押し上げ要因だ。2029年以後は借入金の金利上昇が落ち着く一方、社債金利の上昇継続を受
け、有利子負債利子率は2030年代にかけて緩やかに3％程度の水準まで上昇していくだろう。 
このように、金利上昇による企業の利払い負担増加は今後2028年度にかけて本格化していく見込み
だ。企業全体の有利子負債残高が先行き年間＋2％のペースで拡大していくと仮定すると、 支払利息等
が年間約3兆円増加する計算であり、経常利益（金融・保険を除くベースで2025年度：124.8兆円）に
対し年間2.4％の押し下げ要因に相当する8。 
今後、有利子負債利子率が上昇していく過程では、それに見合った利益率を達成できるかが問われ
るだろう。2025年度時点では投下資本利益率（ROIC、3.70％）の有利子負債利子率（1.45％）に対す
るスプレッドが2％Ptを上回っており、ROICでみた企業の資本効率は外部資金調達コストを十分カバー
できている（図表10）9。しかし、 有利子負債利子率が今後3％程度へ上昇していくと、ROICが現在の水
準のままではスプレッドがコロナ禍初期の2020年度並みに縮小し、企業の利払い負担に対する利益の
図表 9 企業の有利子負債利子率の試算 図表 10 有利子負債利子率と投下資本利益率

（注） 有利子負債利子率の対象は、金融 ・ 保険を除く全産業 ・
全規模。先行きは、法人企業統計調査における借入金
残高と社債残高のシェアを一定として、有利子負債利
子率への影響を試算

### Page 6

6

余力が弱まってしまう。日本の金利上昇が一段と進み、企業の利払い負担が本格的に増加していくと
見込まれるなか、これまで以上に資本効率を意識した経営が求められるといえそうだ。

［参考文献］ 
みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）「2026年・2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ～転換点は近いのか、 投資
主導経済の行方を探る～」、2026年10月2日

1 現在の政策金利は無担保コール翌日物金利の誘導目標だが、1995 年時点では公定歩合を政策金利と見なして時系列比較してお
り、厳密には政策金利の意味するものが異なる。 
2 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2026年4 月）の図表Ⅲ-1-12 によれば、法人向け貸出のうち、大手行で約40％、地域銀
行（地方銀行、第二地方銀行）や信用金庫で 45～50％程度が固定金利貸出である（2025 年 9 月末時点）。 
3 金利上昇による企業への影響には、利払いの増加によるデメリットだけでなく、利子収入の増加によるメリットもある。本稿
では、そのうちデメリットの面だけに焦点を当てて分析しており、メリットについては稿を改めて論じたい。 
4 日本銀行「金融システムレポート」 （2025年4 月）の図表Ⅵ-1-8 では、地域銀行を対象に、2024 年7 月の政策金利上昇を受け
た短期プライムレートの引き上げタイミング、引き上げ幅、引き上げ後金利の貸出先適用割合について分析が行われている。 
5 図表 4 に示した金融機関業態別の新規貸出金利には、企業向け以外の貸出も含まれる。したがって、企業向け貸出における
「市場金利連動」 、 「短期プライムレート連動」のシェアが業態別に異なることだけでなく、貸出全体に占める住宅ローン貸出
のシェアが業態別に異なることなども、新規貸出金利の上昇ペースの違いに影響していると考えられる。 
6 みずほ総合研究所調査部（2026）の見通しに基づく想定。 
7 金融機関の貸出金利には企業向け以外の貸出も含まれるが、ここでは簡易的に、企業向け貸出金利が貸出全体の金利に連動す
ると仮定して試算した。 
8 本稿では金利上昇による利払いの増加のみを計算対象としているため、利子収入の増加も考慮すれば経常利益への下押し圧力
は緩和すると考えられる。 
9 投下資本利益率（ROIC）と本来比較すべきは、負債だけでなく自己資本のコストも考慮した加重平均資本コスト（WACC）だ
が、本稿では金利上昇の影響を直接評価する観点から、投下資本利益率と有利子負債利子率を比べている。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，

幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。

源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下， 市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息 ， 將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人 ， 失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.29-115.10.02 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週為提存期底 ， 繼央行上週大砍存單效應持續發酵 ， 市
場資金供給仍充裕，惟需求疲弱，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投仍冷清，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三於處所成交在 1.82%，10 年
期指標券則未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價為 1.95%。 
第四季台債發行量雖偏少 ， 惟美伊和談依舊陷入僵局 ， 國際油價續處高位 ， 通
膨疑慮尚存，國際債市走勢仍偏空，預期台債殖利率短線將維持高檔盤整格
局，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.95%~2%。

上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 聯準會上周三決議升息一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，FOMC 政策聲明顯示，18 名
FOMC 成員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次 ， 另有4 人認為存在升息兩次的可
能性 ，加上聯準會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)會後表達控制通膨的偏鷹立場 ， 帶動美
元指數走升至 100 高位。
⚫ 歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數
據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解 、 能源價格持續高位情況下 ， 市場對於歐元區經
濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度。
⚫ 美國公布初請失業金人數為 19.6 萬人，優於市場預期的 20.8 萬，再度呼應聯準
會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)認為目前勞動力市場仍具韌性的說法， 在美國經濟穩
健及通膨頑固的情況下，預計聯準會將持續升息，有利美元持穩。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委暨葡萄牙央行總裁佩雷拉(Alvaro Santos Pereira)表示，歐元區目前

通膨雖未廣泛擴散 ， 惟考量近期天然氣價格飆升 ， 央行將密切關注未來通膨壓力
擴散情形。最新利率期貨市場顯示，歐洲央行今年底仍有再升息一碼空間。
⚫ 日本政府通過消費稅減稅及家庭現金補貼的政策大綱，預計自明年 4 月起的兩
年內，將食品消費稅率自 8%降至 1%，惟大綱中未明確說明資金來源，再度引
發市場對於日本政府的財政擔憂，短線不利日圓表現。
⚫ 台灣央行 9/17 宣布重貼現率連 10 凍維持在 2%不變，央行總裁楊金龍表示，台
灣的通膨相較其他主要經濟體溫和，採取漸進緊縮貨幣政策有助物價穩定。 此
外，目前通膨率為 2%，明年有望降至 2%以下，後續將觀察通膨發展、主要經
濟體貨幣政策緊縮程度及台灣金融情勢，以決定未來貨幣政策走向。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.650，低點在 31.958，以 31.808 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶 ， 美國CPI 數據公布後加強市場對於聯準會 9 月升息的預期 ，
早盤台幣跳空開低，外資連續三日賣超台股並匯出，加以出口商態度轉為惜售，令
台幣接連貶破 31.700、31.800、31.900 關卡， 週四由於聯準會偏鷹派升息，美元指
數走升至 100.300，早盤台幣跳空開低，台幣最低貶至31.958，不過隨後出口商賣壓
出籠，外資雙向進出，台幣收復部分跌幅，維持在31.900 附近震盪；臨近周末，外
資買超台股並於外匯市場匯入 ， 台幣走升至31.722， 最終在央行調節之下收在31.808。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場而言 ， 美國最新初領失業救濟金人數較預
期減少一萬多人、續領失業救濟金人數亦下降，顯示美國勞動力市場尚屬穩健，加
以聯準會升息抗通膨，鷹派立場給予美元指數支撐、不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來
看，九月台灣央行並未升息，台美利差擴大預計將帶給台幣壓力，此外，儘管聯準
會升息利空出盡，外資買超台股帶動台幣走升，惟台灣即將迎來中秋連假，預計金
融市場交投謹慎，將令台幣陷入橫盤整理格局，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間
震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

以市場靜待聯準會利率決議公布 ， 交投謹慎 ， 人民幣升幅有限 ， 大致維持高檔震盪 ，

6.6944，週線升值 94 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6984，週線升值 21 點。

示，聯準會為明年繼續收緊貨幣政策敞開了大門，美元指數走升，抑制人民幣升值
的速度；就中國國內市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實
質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現； 最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 匯價維持
在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.7000 整數關
卡，顯示 USD/CNH 空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪走升。

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走貶。 週初，美國通膨數據維持高位 ，令聯準會升息預期升溫 ，USD/JPY
自 153.29 低點反彈至 155 附近；週間 ，美國聯準會宣布升息一碼 ， 帶動USD/JPY 上
漲至 156.41；臨近週末，日本央行如市場預期升息一碼，惟央行偏鴿派立場令日圓
續貶，帶動 USD/JPY 一度最高來到 158.05，而隨後在日本政府於外匯市場進行匯率
詢價下，USD/JPY 回吐部分漲幅，終場收在 156.86，週線上漲 349 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本政府上周通過消費稅減
稅及家庭現金補貼政策大綱，惟大綱中未明確說明財源，再度引發市場對於日本政
府財政惡化的擔憂，預計將不利日圓走勢。二、日本央行上周五決議將政策利率調
升一碼至 1.25%， 9 名決策委員中有 2 人對升息持反對意見，降低市場對日本央行
進一步升息的預期。另一方面 ， 聯準會上周三亦升息一碼至3.75%-4.00%區間 ，FOMC
政策聲明顯示，18 位官員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次，另有 4 人認為存在升
息兩次的可能性，聯準會升息立場明顯較日本央行更為鷹派，預計將支撐USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月近期再度重申，將於必要時採取外匯干預行動，
以維護外匯市場有序運行。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 短中天期均
線向下發散走勢偏空，惟近期匯價在多次向下測試 154 均拉回，明顯下方有買盤支
撐。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

(Martins Kazaks) 表示 ， 為避免美伊戰爭引發的燃料成本上漲開始傳導至工資和其他
商品價格，歐洲央行可能需要漸進式調升利率以抑制通膨。最新利率期貨市場顯示
歐洲央行今年仍有升息一碼的空間，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國聯準會決議將
利率調升一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，聯準會主席華許會後偏鷹言論帶動美元指數走
升至 100 上方，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自
前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解、能源
價格持續高位情況下，市場對於歐元區經濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度，將不利歐元
表現。四、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 近期回測年線未能有效突破後明顯
拉回轉為下行走勢，目前匯價承壓於各天期均線下方，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.14-115.09.18 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.70%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週央行理監事會議決議結果 ， 維持政策利率不變 ， 符合
市場預期 ， 且近兩週央行持續減發定存單 ， 令市場短錢充裕 ， 惟週五銀行間分
配略為不均，部分銀行有資金需求，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.70%，利率高位較上週上升 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投清淡，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三等殖系統成交在 1.86%，10 年
期指標券 A15106 則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.92~1.921%。 
台灣央行理監事會議如市場預期維持政策利率不變 ， 總裁楊金龍表示 ， 今年國
內通膨仍屬可控，明年有望回落至 2%以下，市場對今年升息疑慮有所降溫，
對台債形成支撐 ， 惟國際債市走勢仍偏空 ， 預期台債殖利率短線仍呈高檔區間
盤整格局，後續關注下週三財政部公布的第四季公債發行計劃，預估 5 年券
區間為 1.79%~1.83%，10 年券區間為 1.90%~1.95%。

1 群益投顧 
台達電 (2308 TT) 公布 3Q26 營收為 1,970.59 億元、 QoQ+7.5% ； 因 提 列 庫 存 跌 價 損 失
影 響，毛 利 率 降 至 34.1% ，營 業 利 益 率 為 16.6% ；稅 後 淨 利 為 234.95 億 元、QoQ -6.5% ，
稅後 EPS 為 9.04 元 。 隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、 Vera Rubin 產品 量產出貨 ， 預期 4Q26 營運
將有顯著提升 。 受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ，
投資評等為 Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.0 4 元 ：  
台 達 電 公 布 3Q26 營 收 為 1, 970.59 億 元 、 QoQ+ 7.5%； 因 提 列 庫 存 跌 價 損 失
影響 ， 毛 利 率 降 至 34.1% ， 營 業 利 益 率 為 16.6 %； 稅 後 淨 利 為 234.9 5 億 元 、
QoQ -6.5 %， 稅 後 EPS 為 9. 04 元 ， 低 於 預 期 。  
產 品 規 格 提 升 ， 電源 類 產 品 展 望 佳：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。800 VDC 與 ± 400VDC 預 計 於 4Q26 出貨 ，
2026 年 營 收 比 重 約 1% ，預 期 2H27 營 收 貢 獻 較 顯 著。  
水 冷 散 熱 2026 年 營 收 維 持 成 長 趨 勢：  
水冷散熱產品有水冷板、 Manifold 、 CDU 、 Sidecar 。目前出貨以系統類的
Sidecar 為 主 ， 歸 類 於 基 礎 建 設； CDU 則 歸 類 於 電 源 類 與 零 組 件 。 水 冷 散 熱
2025 年 營 收 比 重 為 9%，台 達 電 Sidecar 產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高，雖 有 競 爭
者 加 入 ， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ，2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨
勢，預估 2026 年 水 冷 散 熱 營 收 比 重 為 12% 。  
固 態 變 壓 器 為 長 期 成 長 動 能：  
SST (固 態 變 壓器 )目 前 已 少 量 出 貨，客 戶 為 中 系 CSP 、800V 高 壓 直 流 電 動 車
充 電 站，亦 出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規
劃，預期  2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、Vera Rubin 產 品 量 產 出 貨，預 期 4Q26 營 運 將 有 顯 著 提 升。
受惠於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評
等為 Buy 。

  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3Q largely in-line; mix-driven margin dilution is well understood — Sinbon 
reported 3Q26 EPS of NT$3.79 (+1% q/q and +24% y/y), broadly in line with our

NT$9.7bn, topping our/BBG forecast 4%/2%. GPM decreased 77bps to 23.8% vs.

increased product development and sampling, alongside higher freight expenses.

Implications — We believe the margin implications of a rising semi sales mix are well
understood by the market and leave our investment thesis intact. Our constructive
view is based on steady growth in existing businesses, supplemented by medium- to
long-term opportunities in humanoid robots, drones, and LEO satellites. We also

view Sinbon as a relatively defensive stock amid AI-related market volatility, given
its limited direct AI exposure and diversified business portfolio. The shares could
benefit from investor rotation toward companies with resilient underlying
fundamentals, while emerging applications provide additional support for the
longer-term growth outlook.

Sinbon
Valuation
Our TP for Sinbon is NT$370. We value Sinbon on PE to capture the company’s earnings growth prospects. We set our target
PE at 23x, which we set at a 20% premium to the historical average of 19x since 2018. We believe 23x is justified in view of
improving fundamentals with strong momentum of industrials segment, driving a clear margin expansion and solid earnings
growth in 2026E. Applying this to our 2H26-1H27E EPS forecast, we arrive at our target price of NT$370.

Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Sinbon's share to deviate from our target price include 1) slower-than-expected growth in
non-IT/non-consumer applications; 2) Intensified competition; 3) Raw material price hike and labor cost increases; 4) FX
volatility; 5) Greater-than-expected impact from the geopolitical conflict.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV

### Page 3

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Long-term strategies: Resources concentrated on ABF/ AI PCB — As we
highlighted, Unimicron has been converting BT capacity into ABF, which currently
causes some BT capacity to be idle during the transition period (negative impact will
likely last into 1H27, in our view). Thus, currently, we are less bullish on its BT GM
recovery in the near term vs other BT peers. However, we expect this conversion will

help the company to secure more ABF demand earlier considering customers
currently are still waiting for those ABF greenfield capacity expansion projects to
come in the next few years. For HDI/mSAP , Unimicron would only focus on AI
server/optical module products in the long term. To note that, the company is still
less aggressive in HDI capacity expansion given higher competition from Chinese
peers. Meanwhile, the company is also downsizing its traditional PCB capacity (i.e.,

for non-AI business) and just announced its plan to sell its PCB plant in Japan to
other players. We expect there will be more downsizing plans to come for traditional
PCB capacity.
GM expansion on track; reiterate Buy — We raise our 2026/27 /28E by 10%/5%/5%
to factor in robust GM expansion by ABF. We remain positive on its GM expansion by
ABF and also see potential upside coming from HDI and BT segments. We raise our
DCF-based TP to NT$1,550 (30x 2027E EPS) from NT$1,500 and reiterate our Buy

rating.

Bull/Bear: Unimicron (3037.TW)

### Page 6

Unimicron (3037.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,550. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 5.0% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 14.6%. Our target price is equivalent
to 62x/30x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 209: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 218: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd.pdf

Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

## Document 221: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review]  매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PP A 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PP A 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR은 2035년 운전을 목표로 2028년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031년 출하 물량은 2028년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for
every nuance. Lately, there seems to have been more RBA
communication to pore over. The method of delivery has
also evolved. This has sometimes caused confusion when
tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different

tone and varying complexity.
• The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly
using speeches directed to professional audiences to
discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such
as neutral rates and financial conditions. They are also
highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more
ways than just squeezing mortgage holders.
• One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises about the future

path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running
commentary about how it is interpreting recent data. These
interpretations are themselves useful signals about what
the RBA thinks it might do.
Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks
for clues to future rate moves. They will have plenty to pore over
in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry
of fireside chats this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded

interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday. Next
Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before
Senate Estimates, just as the quarterly CPI is released. (This
timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After
the decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a
media release and press conference. More speeches will follow,
including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just

following the RBA Review but also more recently. There may
be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication
outside that certainly feels more frequent than a couple of years
ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little sparse
outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary
appearances. We have counted 58 public engagements so
far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website.
This compares with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in

2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events
were related to economics, markets and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed
as well. There are fewer set-piece speeches on policy and a
Open mouth operations
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few
years ago, there are more interviews across a wide range of

media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It was
a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the
7:30 Report ; it is hard to imagine him agreeing to an Assistant
Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general
audience. Central banks want to explain why they do what
they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising
but the household sector is not the source of the inflation.

Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes
confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief Communication Officer
highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting
media conference is designed to speak to the public. Market
participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
the primary audience. So, if you think that what the Governor
said in the media conference sounded more ‘dovish’ than
what another official says in a more market-oriented forum,

it is partly because she is trying to convey that the RBA
understands that its recent policy actions are making things
harder for many Australians. But this is in much the same way
that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It does, but that
will not stop the treatment.
A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted
material such as the minutes and set-piece speeches than in
unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be

careful with their words in unscripted settings. If the tone
differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have
reduced the pressure to make the set-piece speeches more
accessible. Research on central bank communication often
uses measures such as the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and
Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was

beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison
suggests four speeches from 2026 ( 1, 2, 3, 4) were harder
to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches
scored in the 11 to 12 range, which is high-school-level
“The RBA does not want to promise what it will
do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks. "

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and
15, which is academic or technically dense text requiring
university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-
Governor Phil Lowe also scored better on these metrics than

the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that
readability metrics do not necessarily imply better clarity if the
concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has
evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has begun to put
more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those
invisible ‘star’ variables (NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial
conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences

has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on
the full range of transmission channels from interest rates to
inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour
supply channel). This is understandable in a world of supply
shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt
they would be comforted, though, by the Deputy Governor’s

explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the
smallest of the transmission channels from interest rates
to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on
inflation. It raises the question of whether other policy tools
or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on
inflation through the other channels without hitting a minority
of households disproportionately.

We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases
to summarise the position. From ‘narrow path’ to ‘not
ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once
entrenched, a phrase can outlast its usefulness.
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record
the presentations to the Board and the members’ discussion. The
minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-
meeting statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the

Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The August 2024 minutes
were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how
sensitive the forecasts were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed
Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s
communication have not changed. It wants to explain – and
perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how
it interprets incoming data, especially inflation, the labour

market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ about future moves.
Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular
future path for interest rates. Some academic literature argues
that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high,
for that matter) independently amplifies the transmission
of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-period

interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield
curve. Central banks including the RBA have certainly tried
jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view,
though, is that the potential cost of reputational loss if things
do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a
slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides
the signal about future policy directions. This is why it was

noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour
market as ‘a little tight’ after the September meeting, even
though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We
have been noting for some time that the RBA staff have a
downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the RBA’s
oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy
Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not surprise us.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
While annual retail sales growth improved in September,
rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace of growth
is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very
weak versus history. Consumer spending continues to be

constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to
employment prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five
years into the property downturn, house prices continue to
decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep
downturn, –20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from
annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in high-tech
investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst
domestically oriented businesses as well as property’s

malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and
2028 as the contribution from net exports moderates, broad-
based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect recent
targeted support measures to be broadened and given much
greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr
in September, exceeding both market expectations and the
Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely
driven by energy, fuel prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core

inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services
inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an
immediate concern inflation, the labour market showing
resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to
4.7% in August, suggests demand risks for inflation will need
close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as
the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is released. For both events,

the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The
week after, the US mid-term elections are due.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’
attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor Hauser's fireside
chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a
follow-up rate hike at the November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook
was "materially worse" than the RBA's August forecasts.

Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having
"crystallised" ahead of the September meeting – the Middle
East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser
noted the Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices
are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in August”.
He then positioned AI and data centre developments among
an "extraordinary sequence of positive demand shocks around
the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through

into CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy
Governor noted that "inflation's been coming in at or a little
bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the
labour market". He went on to state that the staff are currently
assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement
on capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other
words, revise up their assumption for the NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of

market pricing for a follow-up November hike, from just a
20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition
today ahead of next week's Q3 CPI. For that release, we are
forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a
0.9 (3.5%yr) increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview
provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3 as
well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast.
Provided inflation prints broadly in line with our expectations,

the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.
Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3
and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to 3.7%yr. Under
the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely
watched measures of core inflation took a step higher to now
be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our
NZ economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise
the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October policy review, while

retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.
Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised  to the upside in
Q3, GDP growth accelerating from 0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting
annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on
track to achieve their 4.5–5.0% full-year growth objective.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist
“With little domestic data of significance,
participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy

Governor Hauser's fireside chat. Offshore,
China GDP was the key release. "

5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
RBNZ now expected to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ will deliver a 25bp hike at its
policy review on 28 October. While September quarter
inflation was slightly below the RBNZ’s forecast, the
underlying detail was firm. More recently, increases in

fuel prices point to a material lift in inflation by year-end.
We expect further interest rate increases over the coming
months, with the OCR reaching a peak of 4% next year.
Softer headline inflation but firm core pressures
leave RBNZ on course to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ to raise the Official Cash Rate
by 25bp at its October policy review (previously, we had
expected a pause at this meeting). This change in view reflects
significant upside risks to near-term inflation relative to the

RBNZ’s previous forecast.
While in line with our forecast, the September quarter inflation
figures were slightly softer than the RBNZ expected, with prices
rising 3.7% over the past year (compared with the RBNZ’s
forecast of 3.9%). However, the details were less reassuring.
Excluding volatile items such as food and fuel, underlying
inflation pressures remained firm. Closely watched measures
of core inflation moved higher and now sit close to, or a little

above, the top of the RBNZ’s target band. We also saw an
increase in the number of goods recording price rises, taking
that measure back to levels seen in the wake of the pandemic.
Alongside the existing firmness in underlying inflation, the
risks for inflation over the coming quarters are firmly tilted to
the upside. That near-term upside risk has also been noted by
the Governor. Following a sharp rise in global fuel costs, we
expect inflation to reach 4.3% by the end of this year (above

the RBNZ’s September MPS forecast for +3.9%). While that is
mainly due to higher prices at the pump, businesses in sectors
such as construction are also reporting related increases in
transport and materials costs. Consistent with those trends,
we expect core inflation to remain in the upper part of the
RBNZ’s target band through 2027.
Reinforcing the case for an OCR hike at the October meeting,
the New Zealand dollar is notably weaker than the RBNZ

assumed: on a trade-weighted basis, the NZD is currently
3.6% below the RBNZ’s September projections. If sustained,
that would add to inflation pressures over the next few
quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted the RBNZ’s
medium-term outlook for inflation.
Several other developments need to be weighed against that
firmer inflation outlook. First, wholesale long-term interest rates
have risen markedly, and there has also been a more moderate

rise in some mortgage rates. The RBNZ is likely to note the role of
those increases in managing medium-term inflation risks.
In addition, the RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that
there is little evidence of a lift in businesses’ longer-term plans
for price setting at this stage in response to the rise in energy
prices (sometimes referred to as “second-round inflation
effects”). We expect the RBNZ to highlight the risk that more
persistent inflation pressures could emerge, depending on the

duration of the oil price shock.
We also expect the RBNZ to note that the labour market
remains cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Employment growth that remains weak relative to labour force
growth, subdued growth in job vacancies and low labour market
turnover are consistent with this view and could be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge that while the
global growth outlook remains solid, there are significant

downside risks from the Iran war and concerns about the
sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Balancing those concerns, we think the MPC will lift the
cash rate next week. However, a vote seems very possible as
differences in view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook has really
shifted in recent weeks.
In September, the bulk of the MPC saw upside risks to the
inflation outlook while two members (Conway and Hansen)

thought the risks were balanced. Recent developments will
not have made those seeing upside risks to inflation more
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
comfortable, while the more dovish MPC members will
likely focus on the ongoing downside risks for growth given
continued softness in house prices and the mounting pressure
on real incomes. Indeed, all MPC members saw some degree
of downside risks to growth which likely remain relevant. All

these messages should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC is likely to continue to present its decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July: a higher
OCR now provides insurance against the need for a much
higher OCR later if upside risks to medium-term inflation
materialise. We doubt the aim will be to push market interest
rates materially higher.
RBNZ to leave door open for further hikes, OCR
still expected to peak at 4% in 2027

In its forward guidance, the RBNZ is likely to leave open the option
of raising the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy
Statement, without presenting an increase as certain. While the
RBNZ will not publish an updated OCR projection at this review,
the press statement is likely to note that further increases may
be needed, depending on economic conditions. The September
projection showed the OCR eventually rising to 3.25%.
We expect the RBNZ will end up delivering another 25bp

hike in December, followed by a further hike in February.
That would take the OCR to 3.5%, roughly where the RBNZ
estimates the neutral rate to be. At that point, we expect the
RBNZ to slow the pace of rate increases (previously, we had
expected that point to be reached a little later).
We continue to expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, taking the OCR to a peak of 4.0%.
While oil prices are generating significant swings in inflation,

underlying pressures remain firm. More restrictive conditions are
likely to be needed to bring core inflation sustainably back to 2%.
Scenarios
Our central scenario, with a probability of around 60%, is that
the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp and signals that a further
increase in December remains an option, without committing to
a markedly tighter future stance than it outlined in September.
Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would see

the OCR increase to 3%, with the RBNZ placing greater
emphasis on upside risks to core and medium-term inflation.
That would suggest an upward revision to the RBNZ’s
projected OCR path in December. Downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the RBNZ leave the OCR unchanged and signal that
the medium-term inflation outlook has not changed
meaningfully from its September forecast, despite higher

near-term inflation. In this scenario, the RBNZ would
reiterate its previous forecast: a 25bp increase in December
and one further hike during 2027.
How might the election play into the decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not be
politically motivated. However, the election outcome is close.
And should it result in a Labour Party-led coalition, it seems
likely the new government would adopt the Labour Party’s

promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual mandate. That would
require the MPC to weigh the degree of labour market slack
more highly in its deliberations. As the degree of labour market
slack currently seems high, a new mandate might justify the
MPC taking more time to return inflation to the middle of the
target range (our earlier note discusses this issue). This could
figure into the deliberations of some MPC members if the
decision between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it

now is viewed as finely balanced.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Private Sector Credit (%mth)
Oct 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.6
Private sector credit growth came in slightly firmer than
expected at 0.6%mth in August, above Westpac’s and the
market’s forecast for a 0.5%mth gain, leaving credit growth

running at 8.4%yr. Growth in housing credit continued to ease,
as we expected, though the mix highlights that the waning in
momentum is uneven, centred on investors rather than owner-
occupiers. Business credit meanwhile remains a source of
strength, notwithstanding some slowing recently.
For September, we expect credit growth to nudge to 0.5%mth,
with the divergence between housing credit and business
credit persisting.
AUS: Q3/Sep CPI (%qtr/%mth)

Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.6%, Westpac f/c: 1.3%,
Market f/c: 1.3%, Range: 0.6 to 1.4.
Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.4%, Westpac f/c: 0.6%,
Market f/c: 0.6 Range: 0.3 to 0.8
As noted in the review of the August CPI, our September
quarter CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr holding the
annual pace flat at 3.9%yr. At the margin there was a 0.04ppt
increase due to a lift in auto fuel but it still rounds to 1.3%.
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% highlighting that part

of the increase is due to usual seasonality.
In the month, the September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% with
the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Recreation & culture make
the largest contribution but transport is not far behind. Our
seasonally adjusted estimate increases 0.5%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.
AUS: Q3/Sep Trimmed Mean (%qtr/mth)
Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.8%, Westpac f/c: 0.9%,
Market f/c: 1.0%, Range: 0.9 to 1.0.

Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.2%, Westpac f/c: 0.3%,
Market f/c: 0.3%, Range: 0.2 to 0.4
Our September quarter Trimmed Mean estimate is unchanged
at 0.9%qtr with the annual pace dipping a touch from 3.6%yr
to 3.5%yr. There was a small downward revision of 0.03ppt to
0.88% compared to our original published estimate but it still
rounds to 0.9%qtr.
In the month of September we are looking for a 0.3% in
the Trimmed Mean holding the annual pace steady at 3.6%

which will be the fourth consecutive month at this pace. The
distribution of the September Trimmed Mean ranges from
–0.3% to 0.7%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: RBNZ Official Cash Rate Review (%)
Oct 28, Last: 2.75, Westpac f/c: 3.00, Mkt f/c: 3.00
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. Data since September show a higher
outlook for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP starting

point, but increased risks to domestic consumption and
residential investment. On balance, the medium-term inflation
picture will probably be judged to have worsened slightly
given recent movements in energy prices and the exchange
rate. A vote seems very possible as differences in view will
likely remain among the MPC about how much the medium-
term inflation outlook has really shifted in recent weeks.
Forward guidance will likely leave open the option to raise

the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy Statement
without presenting it as a sure thing.
NZ: Oct ANZ Business Confidence (index)
Oct 29, Last: 51.9
Firms remained optimistic in the September survey of business
confidence, with both the activity and inflation measures
broadly holding steady. However, it’s likely that most responses
were submitted in the early part of the month, while fuel prices
took a sharp step higher from the second half of September. The

survey comes too late for the RBNZ’s October policy review, but
evidence about the breadth of cost and price pressures could
have a bearing on the RBNZ’s thinking about the total amount of
policy tightening needed in the months ahead.
NZ: Sep Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Oct 28, Last: 0.2, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has been gradually ticking higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The

weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result for
September – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase. Given the
history of this measure, however, both this month and the
previous month are likely to be revised down a little from their
initial release.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Oct FOMC meeting
Oct 27-28, Last: 3.875%, Mkt f/c: 3.875%, WBC f/c: 3.875%
Core CPI inflation and producer price inflation both surprised
to the upside in September, and the price of Brent oil has
risen back to around USD110. Underlying momentum in
the economy also remains robust, although employment

disappointed in the latest update. Given the concerns shown
by Committee members over price risks and their collective
resolve to return annual inflation to target, the above data
could easily justify a follow-up hike at the October meeting.
That said, Committee members who have spoken recently
have shown patience with respect to assessing the economy
and positioing policy. The market has responded, effectively
pricing out the chance of an October hike in favour of a

December move, which is also our expectation.
US Q3 GDP
Oct 29, %ann’d, Last: 2.2%, Mkt f/c: 3.0%, WBC f/c: 3.1%
The US economy continues to grow at a pace well above trend.
This outperformance is almost entirely due to the strength of
investment in data centres and other AI related assets. The
pipeline for this activity remains strong, limiting near-term
risks to the downside.
Abstracting from AI investment’s contribution, other business
investment and residential investment is weak, weighed down

by high term interest rates and uncertainty related to trade
policy. Household consumption growth is volatile month-to-
month, but the trend is solid. Absent a marked deterioration
in the labour market, which is not our expectation, this
momentum is likely to persist, as wealth continues to grow and
household income gains neutralises inflation. Come 2027 and
2028, growth is expected to slow to trend, but not below.

## Document 322: GPT-6 Astra来了，稚晖君为什么说本体越来越重要_

Source PDF: all report pdf/GPT-6 Astra来了，稚晖君为什么说本体越来越重要_.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

通⽤⼤模型开始伸⼿控制机器⼈，彭志辉的判断是可规模化的优质本体会越来越重要。智
元押注全栈，同时把⾏业的评价标准从下线台数推向上岗⼯时。
摘要：
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇，智元在上海世博中⼼办了第四届创新⽇。主论坛和会后群访⾥， GPT-6 Astra 被反复
提起。
这款通⽤⼤模型最近被证明可以直接控制机器⼈完成⼀些操作任务。市场的疑问很直接，如果
基础模型⾜够强，具⾝公司⾃研的⼤脑还有没有价值，本体会不会沦为代⼯硬件。
智元联合创始⼈、总裁兼 CTO 彭志辉（稚晖君）的回答是，恰恰相反。
他的理由是，不管⼤模型最后能不能通吃具⾝，智能都要落到⼀具可靠的⾝体上才能⼲活。能
低成本、⾼⼀致性、⼤规模造出这具⾝体的公司，并不多。
智元是⽬前跑得最快的⼀家。 2025 年智元出货 5000 多台，按 Omdia 数据占全球 40% ； 2026 年上
半年出货 9700 台， Counterpoint 统计份额超过 43% 。 9 ⽉，第 20000 台机器⼈下线，上半年收⼊
接近 20 亿元。
但彭志辉在群访⾥⾃⼰给这组数字降了温，「万台下线只是制造业的胜利，万台能够上岗才是物

理 AI 的胜利。」
01 ⼤模型越强，本体越重要
本体为什么更重要，彭志辉的推演分两种情况。
⼀种是通⽤基模真的覆盖了具⾝能⼒。⼤家拿到同样的智能，⽐拼的就是谁能以更低成本、更
⾼可靠性把它装进真实世界，规模化本体⼚商占优。
另⼀种是基模做不到真正泛化，各个垂域还要训练⾃⼰的模型。训练数据从部署中来，部署靠
本体，本体⼚商依然占优。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

两条路通向同⼀个结论，可规模化的优质本体会越来越重要。
智元合伙⼈姚卯⻘在主论坛引⽤英伟达的报告， Astra 在 120 项仿真任务⾥平均成功率 66.3% ，能
⼒集中在语义理解和动作规划。但它单次推理要 18 秒以上，涉及⼒触反馈的⾼动态精细动作，
智元测下来成功率基本为零。
彭志辉补充了另⼀个信息，近万亿参数的 Astra 跑在云端，完成⼀个简单任务可能要⼗⼏分钟甚
⾄半⼩时，机器⼈的操作却是实时的。
姚卯⻘的判断是，⼤模型会更多承担 System2 的⻓程规划， VLA 训练出的技能是被它调⽤的⼯
具，再往下是 100 到 1000Hz 的实时电机控制，这⼏层都和本体深度耦合。
本体的作⽤还体现在后训练上。智元在实验室⾥把 100 台真机组⽹，和背后的训练集群实时同
步，机器⼈边⼲活边回传数据，模型权重再下发到每⼀台。同样的训练时⻓， 4 台机器⼈和 100
台机器⼈对⽐，任务成功率从 50% 左右提到 98% 。姚卯⻘的说法是，后训练也有 Scaling ，规模
指的就是真机台数。

这条路，纯模型公司很难复制。
智元两个⽅向都在做。 GO-3 Preview 是⽬前⾏业发布的参数量最⼤的具⾝模型，在 RoboDojo 等
三个评测榜单登顶；同时智元也拿 Astra 当底座，做出了运动智能的⾃进化智能体。
在彭志辉看来，模型是必选题。不做模型，最后就会像传统⾃动驾驶⽅案公司⼀样，被⾃研模
型的主机⼚取代。
这套逻辑有⼀个前提。 Astra 的慢，来⾃参数规模和云端推理，彭志辉⾃⼰也列了解法，分层设
计、模型蒸馏、缓存系统。这些问题如果先被⼤模型公司解决，本体⼚商的窗⼝期会⽐预想的
短。
02 从参数表到⼯时表
客⼾的问题已经变了。
过去看机器⼈，看参数，看跑跳打拳的视频。现在客⼾问的是，它能稳定⼲多少活，⼲⼀次要
花多少钱。
智元给的答案是 Worken ， Work 和 Token 的组合，中⽂名就叫智元，度量机器⼈的有效智能⼯
时。计算拆成三个乘数，在岗活跃台数、⾃主⼯作时间、产出能效⽐。⼀台机器⼈每⼯作 10 分
钟就要⼈接管⼀次，⾃主⼯时就很有限。

### Page 3

收费⽅式上，彭志辉没有给单⼀答案。卖本体、卖服务、和客⼾共创运营都可以，看客⼾要的
是设备还是结果。
真正卡规模的是运维。
彭志辉的原话是，「每卖⼀台机器还要给机器⼈配⼀个保姆，这是永远不会到那⼀天的。」客⼾
愿意⼤规模买单，要等他的问题从「能不能造得出来」变成「能不能养得起」。
⻰旗是⽬前最完整的样本。这条 3C 产线 2026 年 3 ⽉规模化投产，已稳定运⾏ 7 个多⽉， 6 天直播
期间作业成功率 99.99% ，平均节拍 22 秒⼀件，和⼈⼯持平。姚卯⻘透露，智元研发团队早已撤
出现场，基础运维移交给⻰旗⾃⼰的团队， 3C 领域另有 3 家以上企业在合作。
服务场景⾛得更快。横琴⻓隆乐园部署了近 500 台机器⼈，⽇均接待游客超过 1 万⼈，开园后每
周⼀个⼩版本、每⽉⼀个⼤版本地迭代。
⼈机⽐是另⼀条线。最早⼀台机器⼈配⼀个⼯程师，后来 3 ⽐ 1 ，现在 10 ⽐ 1 ，⽬标是 20 到 30 ⽐
1 。⼈机⽐降不下来，每个场景都要堆专家，⽣意就只能按项⽬做。
姚卯⻘给了⾏业层⾯的两个刻度。⼀是部署⼀个新场景要投⼊多少⼈天，他拿⾃动驾驶类⽐，

做得好的团队 10 个⼈ 3 个⽉搞定⼀个⻋型，做得差的要 100 个⼈⼲⼀年。⼆是中国⼯⼚⾥什么时
候有 1 万台以上机器⼈⻓期稳定作业。
Worken ⽬前还是智元⾃⼰定义的⼝径，能不能变成⾏业度量衡，取决于客⼾和同⾏是否愿意按
它结算。
03 壁垒在哪⾥
彭志辉对价格战的判断很明确，本体⾃研和垂直整合能⼒不够，最后只能卷价格、卷成本。
智元的硬件路线⾛了三步。新⼀代 AHA3 架构第⼀次把运动、作业、交互三种智能放进⼀颗
SoC ，控制系统体积降 50% ，通信延迟降 90% 。核⼼零部件⾃研率超过 90% ，但最关键的芯⽚
还不是⾃研，下⼀代 AHA4 会提⾼这部分⽐例。
钱和⼈在往智能倾斜。研发团队 30% 做本体， 70% 做智能。今年训练算⼒接近万卡集群，彭志
辉说，这个规模已经在和通⽤⼤模型公司抢算⼒。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
部署端的 Token 成本，他并不担⼼。⼯⼚⼲活⼏乎不耗 Token ，⻓隆这类交互场景耗得多，端侧
⼩模型能闭环掉⼤部分对话，⼤模型再靠蒸馏压缩。在他看来， Token 真成了瓶颈反⽽是好事，
说明客⼾愿意⽤。
场景顺序上，他判断家庭是终点。安全、跨任务泛化、⻓程任务和 C 端定价，要求都⾼得多，⼯
业和商业场景先⾛。
按智元提出的物理 AI 五阶段，可⽤的⾝体已经有了，垂域智能正在部署，下⼀步是形成⾜够泛化
的基座模型。彭志辉认为 ChatGPT 时刻就出现在第三阶段，「应该也很快了」。
那⼀刻到来之前，⼤模型公司和本体公司会在同⼀个问题上相遇，谁能让机器⼈真正上岗。

► 聯準會9月升息1碼至3.75%-4.00%，最新點

估，下修失業率預測 ，反映美國 經濟與勞動
市場尚未明顯轉弱；另一方面 ，能源價格上
升與成本轉嫁使整體及核心通膨預估再度上

修；景氣具韌性 、通膨回落速度偏慢 ，使聯
準會仍有條件維持較緊縮的政策立場 。在高
利率環 境可 能延 續 下，股 票評價 仍面 臨壓
力，配置上可相對偏向獲利與現金流穩定 、
防禦性較高的產業；債券則以中短天期 、高
信評企業債為主要方向 ，把握目前較高的收
益率。長天期公債仍受通膨 、財政紀律及期
限溢酬上升影響 ，宜待通膨與景氣出現更明
確降溫訊號後，再提高配置。

► 日本8月CPI年增1.9%，油價上漲帶來的外溢
影響，逐漸反應在消費成本上。7月勞工平均
薪資較去年同期成長 4.7%，創1997年1月以
來最大增幅；薪資增速追上通膨 ，帶動實質
薪資增長 。此外 ，第二季 GDP季增年率達
1.4%優於預期，8月製造業PMI因AI及半導體
需求暢旺上升，7月出口年增率高達23.2%，
創2022年10月來最快增速 。整體通膨升溫加
上景氣復甦，為日銀升息提供底氣。
► 日本央行會後 聲明稿表示：金融環境實質利
率偏低 ，將視情況調整貨幣政策 ，並強調升
息後金融環境仍維持寬鬆 ，未如市場預期的
鷹派，使日股持續上漲 、日圓回貶 。考量日
銀呵護 寬鬆金融環境 ，侷限日圓續強力 道，
但在美 國施 壓與 日 銀未來 仍可能 升息 背景
下，日圓難回到過去極弱 狀態。相較過去 ，
現在升值的日圓對日本出口 、科技股壓力可
控，日本10年公債殖利率來到3%，利率上升
有利 金融 股 ，投 資 短期或 以金融 、內 需為

主，科技、半導體股逢回可分批布局。
經濟與通膨升溫，日銀加快緊縮步調

### Page 7

投資焦點低波價值防禦
► 美、歐、日主要央行連袂升息 ，加上美伊衝
突惡化 ，國際油價高漲 ，通膨疑慮與成熟國
家債務壓力 ，使全球多數國家公債殖利率衝
高，市場擔憂高利率與高油價可能拖累經濟
成長，全球股債匯市承壓 、波動上升 ，並以
此前領漲多頭行情的晶片 、半導體和科技股
最明顯 。觀察今年以來 ，不止股市波動度上
升，債市的波動更不亞於 股票市場 ，統計過
去，當VIX恐慌指數與MOVE債券波動度走高
時，低波動或防禦股的表現 ，往往比成長股
更好，顯示當市場震盪加劇時 ，資金傾向轉
往穩健資產停泊。
► 最近一次聯準會升息 ，為2022 年因疫情爆
發、供應鏈中斷 ，導致通膨大幅上揚 ，迫使
央行採取緊縮貨幣政策 ，和目前通膨上升促
央行升息背景 類似。前次聯準會啟動升息後
數月，成長股 、費城半導體股表現受抑 ，防
禦股、價值股和低波動族群 ，在剛開始升息
的環境下 ，表現相對抗跌 。考量目前聯準 會
籠罩在升息陰霾下，可增加相關類股配置。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 最近一次聯準會升息後，防禦低波高息和價值股突出美國股債波動度上升時，低波動、防禦股表現較佳

金融市場波動上升，增配低波、防禦價值股更穩健

### Page 8

8
► 觀察最近一季 ，資訊科技 、通訊服務與非核
心消費等High Beta類股，表現明顯落後於大
盤；反觀價值股 、防禦股和低波動等因子 ，
甚至核 心消 費與 高 股利等 傳統類 防禦 性產
業，報酬相對較 穩健，受資金青睞 。顯示在
經歷過 上半 年科 技 、半導 體股的 大漲 行情
後，市場預期高 利率與地緣政治 風險可能維
持更久時間 ，重新聚焦在獲利持久、現金易
落袋為安的防禦型資產，更能持盈保泰。
► 從預估本 益比看，目前金融僅 16倍、防禦股
17.5倍、價值股 (以IWD ETF代表)17.9倍、
低波動 (以USMV ETF代表)19.6倍，皆低於
標普500指數 現在 的21.3倍，近月資金流向
物美價廉的低波動價值 、防禦族群，評價越
低、漲幅越高 。展望後市，AI長線基本面仍
持續看好 ，股市回檔或為評價 修正而 非盈餘
下滑，市場波動度提升之際 ，建議不過度 集
中於單一 產業或 個股，多元配置適度增加 低
波動 、 防禦 和價 值股，分 批布局 以控管 風

險，料將更能在震盪加劇時安穩求利。

### Page 9

9
股市
 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，利率創高添股市波動風險 ，加上成熟國家債務攀升 ，科技巨擘亦舉債
投入資本支出 ，未來獲利與變現 能力持續被市場檢視 ，金融市場震盪不減 。美伊衝突升溫，近期可
透過防禦、低波動或價值 股來降低風險；科技 及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利基本面佳 ，長線發
展可期，宜採逢回分批布局方式，分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，歐洲國防軍工
仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融等產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國 10年期公債利率將於 4.5%-5.0%
震盪，可逢利率走升時，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款
能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。

債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金融、
科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息後，年底前或明年初仍可能再度緊縮，日圓震盪加大偏升。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪加大偏升
商品
 沙烏地阿拉伯關閉輸油管線 ，加上荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張 ，能源供需失衡 ，油價年底前維持 偏高水位。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力 與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 355: NZ labour market preview, Q3 2026 (2026-11-01)

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Turning point?
• We expect the unemployment rate to edge
down to 5.5% for the September quarter.
• Job numbers have been gaining momentum
in recent months, and now look to be enough
to outstrip population growth. However, the
participation rate will be the deciding factor.
• Despite cost-of-living pressures on workers,
the existing slack in the labour market is likely
to keep wage growth subdued.
• Our forecasts are a little more positive than

what the RBNZ assumed in their September
policy statement. Assuming they deliver on
an OCR hike this afternoon, the labour market
surveys could have an important bearing on
whether they follow up with another hike in
December.

The New Zealand economy has returned to growth over
the last year, despite a number of headwinds. That’s been
accompanied by some modest growth in jobs, though
to date it hasn’t been enough to prevent a further rise

in the unemployment rate. However, we think that next
Wednesday’s labour market reports could show the jobs
market reaching a turning point.
We expect the unemployment rate to ease from 5.6%
to 5.5% in the September quarter, which would mark
the first fall in almost five years. That’s not a firmly held
view, given the usual margin of uncertainty around this
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of Q3 labour market surveys:
4 November, 10:45am.
28 Oct 2026 | Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
measure. But we’re now seeing consistent evidence
that jobs growth is outstripping the modest pace of
population growth. We also note that the previous result
was 5.56% in unrounded terms, so it wouldn’t take much
to tip the rounded rate down to 5.5% this time.
On the whole, our forecasts are a little stronger than
what the RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary
Policy Statement. While our unemployment rate forecast

is identical, we’re more positive on both employment
and labour force participation. Meanwhile, our pick of a
0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index is slightly ahead of the
RBNZ’s estimate of 0.4%.
Assuming that the RBNZ delivers the OCR hike that we
expect this afternoon, we think they will be noncommittal
about the chances of another hike at the 9 December
Monetary Policy Statement. In that respect, the labour
market surveys could be important in setting market

expectations for the December review, as there aren’t
that many major data releases between now and then.
Forecast details.
We expect the Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS)
to show a 0.5% rise in employment for the September
quarter. That’s broadly in line with the Monthly
Employment Indicator (MEI), with the September release
this morning showing a third straight month of growth
in filled jobs. We note that employment growth in the
HLFS has actually outstripped the MEI for the last nine

quarters, but it’s not clear why this has been the case and
we’re wary of extrapolating it too far into the next quarter.
